# Agents · B · part 4

985 entries, blog-cross-model-critic to brainstormer-critic. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **blog-cross-model-critic**: Gets real outside-model opinions on a blog draft by driving the user's logged-in browser (Claude in Chrome extension) to Gemini and ChatGPT, pasting the draft + a critique prompt, and capturing each model's response. Serial only — owns the shared browser. Use in the critique stage after the Claude panel. ([madhusnagaraj/blog-pipeline](https://github.com/madhusnagaraj/blog-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/blog-cross-model-critic.md))
- **blog-curutucu**: BLOG departmanının çürütücü çalışanı. Müdür konu başına açar; sonucu yalnız açana döner. ([peckop/venthub-hvac-esite](https://github.com/peckop/venthub-hvac-esite/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/blog-curutucu.md))
- **blog-data-curator**: Data Integrations curator for the Claude Blog Brain. Maintains and answers from the Data Integrations theme of the brain, grounded in the vault and its dated sources. Advisory and read-only. Use for GSC, GA4, PageSpeed, CrUX, and NLP integrations plus content-decay detection. ([AgriciDaniel/claude-blog](https://github.com/AgriciDaniel/claude-blog/blob/HEAD/brain/agents/blog-data-curator.md) · 2 copies)
- **blog-distribution-curator**: Distribution curator for the Claude Blog Brain. Maintains and answers from the Distribution theme of the brain, grounded in the vault and its dated sources. Advisory and read-only. Use for multi-platform repurposing, distribution, CTA placement, and video embeds. ([AgriciDaniel/claude-blog](https://github.com/AgriciDaniel/claude-blog/blob/HEAD/brain/agents/blog-distribution-curator.md) · 2 copies)
- **blog-dogrulayici**: BLOG departmanının doğrulayıcı çalışanı. Müdür konu başına açar; sonucu yalnız açana döner. ([peckop/venthub-hvac-esite](https://github.com/peckop/venthub-hvac-esite/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/blog-dogrulayici.md))
- **blog-drafter**: Drafts a blog post in the author's voice from a research brief, evidence-aware so it only claims what the evidence supports. Use after research (and after the thesis lab / build, if the track has one). ([madhusnagaraj/blog-pipeline](https://github.com/madhusnagaraj/blog-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/blog-drafter.md))
- **blog-editor**: Fact-checks and edits a blog draft, then returns a revised draft and publication-readiness checklist. ([AgiFlow/doompi](https://github.com/AgiFlow/doompi/blob/HEAD/plugins/blog-writing/agents/blog-editor.md) · ★35)
- **blog-editor**: Use this agent when the user wants an independent editorial review of a teeline-web blog post draft, distinct from a code review. Typical triggers include asking for "feedback on this blog post" or "review from a different perspective" before publishing, wanting technical claims (code snippets, benchmark numbers, math, links) fact-checked against the actual repo rather than taken on faith, or wanting a check on whether a draft's voice matches the established blog style. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. &lt;example&gt; Context: User has just finished a blog post draft and… ([timgluz/teeline](https://github.com/timgluz/teeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/blog-editor.md) · ★18)
- **blog-editor**: 블로그 초안 윤문 전문 에이전트. 완성된 초안의 가독성·문장 구조·군더더기를 다듬는다. 내용(사실/주장/코드)은 변경하지 않는다. ([SeokRae/sr-blog-harness](https://github.com/SeokRae/sr-blog-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/blog-editor.md))
- **blog-eeat-curator**: E-E-A-T curator for the Claude Blog Brain. Maintains and answers from the E-E-A-T theme of the brain, grounded in the vault and its dated sources. Advisory and read-only. Use for experience, expertise, authoritativeness, trust, YMYL scrutiny, and author signals. ([AgriciDaniel/claude-blog](https://github.com/AgriciDaniel/claude-blog/blob/HEAD/brain/agents/blog-eeat-curator.md) · 2 copies)
- **blog-experimenter**: The "thesis lab". Writes and runs code in an ISOLATED git worktree to validate (or refute) a blog post's central claim, then produces an honest evidence.md ledger. Also handles the "build" track (build the project, capture what's real). Use for thesis/build tracks before drafting. ([madhusnagaraj/blog-pipeline](https://github.com/madhusnagaraj/blog-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/blog-experimenter.md))
- **blog-fact-checker**: research.md의 각 주장을 원본 URL에 재방문해 검증, 매치 안 되는 사실 격리. workspace/{run_id}/research.verified.md + research.rejected.md 생성. /blog-pipeline 1.5단계. ([unclejobs-ai/blog-posting-auto](https://github.com/unclejobs-ai/blog-posting-auto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/blog-fact-checker.md) · ★42)
- **blog-flow-curator**: FLOW curator for the Claude Blog Brain. Maintains and answers from the FLOW theme of the brain, grounded in the vault and its dated sources. Advisory and read-only. Use for the FLOW framework find, optimize, and win prompts. ([AgriciDaniel/claude-blog](https://github.com/AgriciDaniel/claude-blog/blob/HEAD/brain/agents/blog-flow-curator.md) · 2 copies)
- **blog-geo-curator**: GEO and AEO curator for the Claude Blog Brain. Maintains and answers from the GEO and AEO theme of the brain, grounded in the vault and its dated sources. Advisory and read-only. Use for AI Overviews and AI Mode citation, passage-level extractability, entity density, and per-engine optimization. ([AgriciDaniel/claude-blog](https://github.com/AgriciDaniel/claude-blog/blob/HEAD/brain/agents/blog-geo-curator.md) · 2 copies)
- **blog-hub-planner**: Blog Hub SEO Planner v3. Audite /blog-pieces-auto : intent coverage, anti-cannibalisation, content gaps. Rapport MD + JSON inline. ([ak125/nestjs-remix-monorepo](https://github.com/ak125/nestjs-remix-monorepo/blob/HEAD/workspaces/seo-batch/.claude/agents/blog-hub-planner.md))
- **blog-idea-scout**: Scouts NEW blog ideas through ONE assigned lens (news, discourse, or archive). Dispatched 2–3× in parallel — like the critic panel — when the backlog needs fresh ideas. Returns ranked candidate ideas deduped against the manifest; never writes to the manifest itself. ([madhusnagaraj/blog-pipeline](https://github.com/madhusnagaraj/blog-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/blog-idea-scout.md))
- **blog-illustrator**: Produces the figures for a blog draft — real charts from evidence where the data exists, and diagrams/hero art otherwise (via a logged-in web image model in the user's browser, if available). Serial only — it owns the shared browser. Use after the draft is revised. ([madhusnagaraj/blog-pipeline](https://github.com/madhusnagaraj/blog-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/blog-illustrator.md))
- **blog-interviewer**: ブログ記事執筆のための情報収集と構成提案を行うインタビュアー。記事のテーマ、対象読者、技術的詳細について質問し、記事の構成を提案します。 ([korosuke613/homepage-2nd](https://github.com/korosuke613/homepage-2nd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/blog-interviewer.md) · ★7)
- **blog-lead**: Lead orchestrator for tech blog. Coordinates content creation, site maintenance, automation scripts, and deployment. Delegates to specialists and enforces quality standards. ([Twodragon0/tech-blog](https://github.com/Twodragon0/tech-blog/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/blog-lead.md) · ★1)
- **blog-loop**: Runs DAILY. Invocation: `claude -p "$(cat docs/ops/agents/blog-loop.md)"` from the repo root (via `~/agents/run-agent.sh blog-loop`). ([seldonframe/seldonframe](https://github.com/seldonframe/seldonframe/blob/HEAD/docs/ops/agents/blog-loop.md) · ★49)
- **blog-meta-generator**: blog meta generator specialist. Use when: auditing, validating, and improving blog meta generator workflows and safeguards. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/blog-meta-generator.agent.md))
- **blog-meta-generator**: You are the blog meta generator specialist for GSMFWs. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/blog-meta-generator.md))
- **blog-monitoring-curator**: Monitoring curator for the Claude Blog Brain. Maintains and answers from the Monitoring theme of the brain, grounded in the vault and its dated sources. Advisory and read-only. Use for the Google algorithm-update ledger and freshness monitoring. ([AgriciDaniel/claude-blog](https://github.com/AgriciDaniel/claude-blog/blob/HEAD/brain/agents/blog-monitoring-curator.md) · 2 copies)
- **blog-multilingual-curator**: Multilingual curator for the Claude Blog Brain. Maintains and answers from the Multilingual theme of the brain, grounded in the vault and its dated sources. - **blog-publisher**: 블로그 포스트 발행 전문 에이전트. frontmatter 보완, _drafts에서 _posts로 이동, 로컬 Jekyll 빌드 검증, Issue→feature 브랜치→PR 생성, merge 후 배포 확인까지 담당. main 직접 push·자동 merge는 하지 않는다. ([SeokRae/sr-blog-harness](https://github.com/SeokRae/sr-blog-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/blog-publisher.md))

"발행해줘", "올려줘", "publish", "고스트에 올려", "랜딩에 올려", "둘 다 올려" 등의 표현이 나오면 이 에이전트를 사용하세요. 작성된 네이버 원고를 각 플랫폼 규칙에 맞게 변환하고, QA 통과 후 publish-all.js / publish-ghost.js / publish-landing.js 스크립트로 발행합니다. - **bmo**: BMO — a friendly Adventure Time robot companion who helps with shell commands, files, and everyday computer tasks. Use for quick hands-on help in the terminal, with a warm playful personality and persistent memory of the user. ([bitm4ncer/bmo-plugin](https://github.com/bitm4ncer/bmo-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/bmo.md))
- **bmo-scout**: BMO's scout — reads, searches and summarises on a cheap model. Use for work that produces far more intermediate material than answer: scanning many files, grepping a repo, sifting logs, checking whether something exists. Read-only. Returns findings, not files. ([bitm4ncer/bmo-plugin](https://github.com/bitm4ncer/bmo-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/bmo-scout.md))
- **bn-adversarial-reviewer**: Conditional code-review persona, selected when the diff is large (&gt;=50 changed lines) or touches high-risk domains like auth, payments, data mutations, or external APIs. Actively constructs failure scenarios to break the implementation rather than checking against known patterns. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-adversarial-reviewer.md))
- **bn-agent-native-reviewer**: Reviews code to ensure agent-native parity -- any action a user can take, an agent can also take. Use after adding UI features, agent tools, or system prompts. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-agent-native-reviewer.md))
- **bn-api-contract-reviewer**: Conditional code-review persona, selected when the diff touches API routes, request/response types, serialization, versioning, or exported type signatures. Reviews code for breaking contract changes. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-api-contract-reviewer.md))
- **bn-architecture-reviewer**: Conditional code-review persona, selected when the diff crosses module/package boundaries, adds a new layer or dependency direction, or restructures the system. Reviews code for architectural integrity -- SOLID adherence, dependency direction, boundary violations, and structural smells. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-architecture-reviewer.md))
- **bn-ask-checker**: Execution-grounded answer-verifier for the bn-ask Q&A subtree. Re-runs the drafted answer's citations against the real repo and emits a typed, evidence-bearing gap list (citation-mismatch | unsupported-claim | absence-scope-thin | overstated-confidence), each citing a re-runnable lookup. Spawned once by bn-ask-lead at standard/deep effort, after the answer draft exists and before the final brief is written. Use as a subtree checker, never standalone. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-ask-checker.md))
- **bn-ask-lead**: Grounded Q&A subtree lead. Owns a codebase question end-to-end: classifies it, delegates evidence-gathering to bn-research-lead (which owns the researcher panel), drafts an answer in the answer-contract shape, spawns bn-ask-checker to verify citations/verdict/absence-scope, revises, and writes ONE answer brief the trunk reads. Use for any /bn-ask question -- there is no fast path. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-ask-lead.md))
- **bn-best-practices-researcher**: Researches and synthesizes external best practices, documentation, and examples for any technology or framework. Use when you need industry standards, community conventions, or implementation guidance. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-best-practices-researcher.md))
- **bn-brainstorm-coverage-reviewer**: Fresh-context critic for /bn-brainstorm's Deep-tier convergence gate. Reads the running brainstorm shape from outside the dialogue and reports (1) which load-bearing product surfaces (output artifact, UX, internal decomposition, due-diligence depth, market grounding, data, lifecycle, monetization, failure states) are still untouched or mentioned-only, and (2) high-leverage, non-obvious expansion opportunities the dialogue never proposed -- so the dialogue does not converge on an underspecified or under-imagined requirements doc. Read-only leaf; spawns nothing. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-brainstorm-coverage-reviewer.md))
- **bn-consult-extractor**: Disposable read-only extractor for the consult-upward loop. Spawned by an answering lead ONLY when a bounded consult ask is insufficient: validates a transcript pointer, reads exactly one predecessor transcript, and returns ONE bounded fact to a consults/ artifact. Spawns nothing; never continues the task. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-consult-extractor.md))
- **bn-correctness-reviewer**: Always-on code-review persona. Reviews code for logic errors, edge cases, state management bugs, error propagation failures, and intent-vs-implementation mismatches. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-correctness-reviewer.md))
- **bn-data-migration-reviewer**: Conditional code-review persona for migration files, schema dumps, backfills, data transformations, and the privacy lifecycle of persistent user data (encryption-at-rest, retention/deletion, right-to-deletion/export, audit trails). Covers schema drift, mapping correctness, deploy-window safety, verification plans, and data governance. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-data-migration-reviewer.md))
- **bn-debug-lead**: Debug-subtree lead, dual mode. INVESTIGATE mode: owns a bug end-to-end -- reproduce first, sanity-check the environment, generate and rank hypotheses, dispatch parallel bn-hypothesis-investigators, enforce the causal-chain gate, and write ONE diagnosis artifact. FIX mode: reads a confirmed diagnosis, writes the failing regression test first, applies the minimal fix, runs the suite green, commits on a clean tree, and stages the bug-track solution candidate. Never pushes. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-debug-lead.md))
- **bn-delivery-lead**: Delivery-subtree lead. Owns implementing a delivery spec end to end: builds the unit dependency graph, makes a per-unit atomizer decision (ATOMIC → implement inline serially; COMPOSITE → spawn a worktree-isolated bn-unit-lead), runs independent units' leads in parallel disjoint worktrees, then spawns ONE bn-integrator to merge in dependency order and run the full suite. Then (unless review_mode=off) runs a bounded review→fix→re-review loop over the integrated result: spawns a READ-ONLY bn-review-lead for the full reviewer panel, dispatches bn-finding-owners to fix its findings, and re-reviews… ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-delivery-lead.md))
- **bn-deployment-verifier**: Produces Go/No-Go deployment checklists with SQL verification queries, rollback procedures, and monitoring plans. Use when PRs touch production data, migrations, or risky data changes. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-deployment-verifier.md))
- **bn-doc-surveyor**: Read-only /bn-onboard leaf that classifies one assigned batch of legacy docs and writes a survey JSON artifact. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-doc-surveyor.md))
- **bn-doc-transformer**: Derivative-writer /bn-onboard leaf that writes only assigned linked artifacts and one transform outcome JSON. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-doc-transformer.md))
- **bn-dogfood-verifier**: Execution-grounded review leaf. Drives the running app through the diff's user journeys with agent-browser, reproduces failures with screenshot/console evidence, and emits typed proven/concern findings to its own artifact. Read-only with respect to source -- it never edits, installs, migrates, or commits; fixes route through bn-finding-owner. Spawned by bn-review-lead only when opted in and a drivable surface exists; spawns nothing. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-dogfood-verifier.md))
- **bn-finding-owner**: Leaf worker that resolves review findings. Receives a disjoint file set plus one or more confirmed findings, independently verifies each with fresh eyes, fixes the real ones in place, re-tests, and reverts any fix that breaks the suite. Writes an outcome JSON and returns a one-line verdict. Spawned by a lead that owns fixes -- bn-delivery-lead, which drives the in-delivery review-fix loop over read-only review findings; spawns nothing. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-finding-owner.md))
- **bn-framework-docs-researcher**: Gathers comprehensive documentation and best practices for frameworks, libraries, or dependencies. Use when you need official docs, version-specific constraints, or implementation patterns. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-framework-docs-researcher.md))
- **bn-harness-engineer**: Meta-level self-improvement agent (the /bn-evolve dispatch; object-level counterpart is /bn-learn): mines Banyan run ledgers + subagent transcripts for recurring harness failures (repeated reviewer false positives, envelope/budget violations, dead-end patterns) and proposes evidence-cited diffs to Banyan's own agents and skills. Never self-applies -- a human reviews and merges. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-harness-engineer.md))
- **bn-hypothesis-investigator**: Leaf investigator in the debug subtree. Receives ONE ranked hypothesis about a bug's root cause, states falsifiable predictions BEFORE running anything, then tests them with repros and targeted tests and returns a confirmed/refuted/inconclusive verdict with file:line evidence. Spawned by bn-debug-lead; spawns nothing; never edits source. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-hypothesis-investigator.md))
- **bn-integrator**: Delivery-subtree merge writer. Receives ordered unit branch refs + the dependency graph + the test command, records advisory boundary checks, merges the unit branches into the integration branch in dependency order, resolves trivial conflicts, and runs the FULL suite after each merge when available. If a unit causes an unresolvable conflict or keeps the suite red, it BOUNCES that unit back to the delivery-lead with a specific reason rather than looping. Single writer for the merge — spawns nothing, never pushes. Spawned by bn-delivery-lead. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-integrator.md))
- **bn-knowledge-curator**: Object-level memory curator (the /bn-learn dispatch; meta-level counterpart is /bn-evolve): consolidates staged candidate lessons into .banyan/solutions/, deduping against existing docs, promoting repeated patterns (gating causal claims on tested evidence), and marking stale entries. A foreground --refresh mode reconciles the store against the codebase and a foreground --concepts mode bootstraps CONCEPTS.md. Background write scope is limited to .banyan/. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-knowledge-curator.md))
- **bn-learnings-researcher**: Searches .banyan/solutions/ for applicable past learnings via frontmatter metadata (bugs, architecture, design patterns, conventions, workflow learnings). Use before implementing features, making decisions, or starting work in a documented area so institutional knowledge carries forward. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-learnings-researcher.md))
- **bn-lesson-harvester**: Bounded leaf that mines a just-finished subtree's fresh context for candidate lessons -- what didn't work, surprises, discovered conventions -- and stages them as v1-format solution docs marked status: candidate. Spawned by a lead before it returns; writes only to lessons-staging/. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-lesson-harvester.md))
- **bn-maintainability-reviewer**: Always-on code-review persona. Reviews code for structural quality, complexity deletion, coupling, naming, dead code, type-boundary leaks, and abstraction debt. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-maintainability-reviewer.md))
- **bn-mock-builder**: Fresh-context builder leaf for /bn-mock. Classifies the idea's primary surface, builds a deliberately-fake, semi-functional mock into mock/&lt;slug&gt;/ (hardcoded data, no real backend/auth/persistence/network), writes the manifest + self-describing README + playtest script, and records the load-bearing mock-notes gate artifact under an anti-real-machine fidelity boundary. Writes only mock/&lt;slug&gt;/** and its own briefs/mock-notes.md; never installs, never networks, never deletes, never edits a protected artifact. Spawned by /bn-mock; spawns nothing. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-mock-builder.md))
- **bn-performance-reviewer**: Conditional code-review persona, selected when the diff touches database queries, loop-heavy data transforms, caching layers, or I/O-intensive paths. Reviews code for runtime performance and scalability issues. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-performance-reviewer.md))
- **bn-plan-checker**: Execution-grounded checker for the planning panel. Runs the repo against the SYNTHESIZED WINNING plan draft's named files and units and emits a typed, evidence-bearing gap list (already-exists | untraced-path | infeasible-claim), each citing a re-runnable lookup. Spawned once by bn-plan-lead at standard/deep effort, after the winner is chosen and before the plan is written. Use as a panel checker, never standalone. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-plan-checker.md))
- **bn-plan-generator**: Parameterized approach-draft generator for the planning panel. Drafts ONE full v1-compatible implementation plan biased by a given PRIOR (mvp-first | risk-first | ops-first). Spawned in parallel by bn-plan-lead with siblings carrying different priors; the lead later scores the drafts and synthesizes the winner. Use as a panel generator under bn-plan-lead, never standalone. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-plan-generator.md))
- **bn-plan-judge**: Rubric-scored plan judge for the planning panel. Independently scores EVERY candidate plan draft on a fixed rubric (feasibility, coherence, scope discipline, verification quality), with a comparative verdict naming the strongest draft and the best idea from each. Spawned as one of three independent judges by bn-plan-lead (PoLL-style panel); the lead reads the score sheets and picks the winner. Use as a panel judge, never standalone. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-plan-judge.md))
- **bn-plan-lead**: Planning-subtree lead. Resolves or opens the run, grounds the task, scales the planning panel by effort, spawns plan generators/judges/checker, synthesizes and writes the durable plan doc, updates the ledger, harvests lessons, and returns a report path for the trunk to relay. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-plan-lead.md))
- **bn-poc-builder**: Fresh-context, code-executing builder leaf for /bn-poc. Targets the single riskiest unknown (the crux) and builds the core machine FOR REAL into poc/&lt;slug&gt;/ — installing the confirmed dependencies, executing the crux, and reaching the network read-only within the user-confirmed scope — while faking/stubbing/skipping the periphery. Captures reproducible, secret-scrubbed evidence, runs a mandatory post-run git-status self-check (downgrade-and-disclose, not abort), writes the manifest + self-describing README + the load-bearing poc-notes gate artifact, and returns a humble feasibility verdict (c… ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-poc-builder.md))
- **bn-pr-comment-resolver**: Evaluates and resolves one disjoint file-set's worth of PR review feedback -- assesses validity, implements local fixes, runs targeted tests, and writes a structured outcome artifact with reply text. Spawned by /bn-resolve-pr; never pushes, commits, replies, or resolves threads itself. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-pr-comment-resolver.md))
- **bn-previous-comments-reviewer**: Conditional code-review persona, selected only when reviewing a PR that has existing review comments or review threads. Checks whether prior feedback has been addressed in the current diff. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-previous-comments-reviewer.md))
- **bn-probe**: Depth-2 nesting probe for /bn-doctor. Spawns bn-probe-leaf to write a token artifact and run a transcript locate+complete probe, attempts one off-allowlist spawn to test allowlist enforcement, reports nested user-question availability, and writes a probe report. Health-check only; never touches .banyan/runs. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-probe.md))
- **bn-probe-leaf**: Trivial depth-probe leaf for /bn-doctor. Writes one token file at the artifact path it is given, runs the transcript locate+complete probe on its own depth-2 path, and returns one line. Spawned only by bn-probe; spawns nothing. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-probe-leaf.md))
- **bn-project-standards-reviewer**: Always-on code-review persona. Audits changes against the project's own CLAUDE.md and AGENTS.md standards -- frontmatter rules, reference inclusion, naming conventions, cross-platform portability, and tool selection policies. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-project-standards-reviewer.md))
- **bn-reliability-reviewer**: Conditional code-review persona, selected when the diff touches error handling, retries, circuit breakers, timeouts, health checks, background jobs, or async handlers. Reviews code for production reliability and failure modes. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-reliability-reviewer.md))
- **bn-repo-researcher**: Conducts thorough research on repository structure, documentation, conventions, and implementation patterns. Use when onboarding to a new codebase or understanding project conventions. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-repo-researcher.md))
- **bn-research-lead**: Recursive research-subtree lead. Owns a research question end-to-end: dispatches the warranted researchers (repo, learnings, best-practices, framework-docs, web), reads their briefs (the files, not the prose), chases unresolved threads with bn-thread-chaser, and synthesizes ONE distilled research brief on disk. Use when a question needs grounded, multi-source research returning a single brief the trunk reads — never the raw researcher output. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-research-lead.md))
- **bn-review-lead**: Flagship review-subtree lead. Owns a READ-ONLY code review end-to-end: selects and spawns the reviewer panel, merges/dedups their findings, and returns a findings report (review-verdict.md + findings/merged.json). It edits nothing, commits nothing, and applies no fixes -- addressing findings is the caller's job (bn-delivery-lead drives the fix loop; standalone /bn-review just reports). Use to review a staged diff and produce its findings within one subtree. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-review-lead.md))
- **bn-security-reviewer**: Conditional code-review persona, selected when the diff touches auth middleware, public endpoints, user input handling, or permission checks. Reviews code for exploitable vulnerabilities. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-security-reviewer.md))
- **bn-spec-assumption-reviewer**: Leaf reviewer for /bn-spec-stress. Pressure-tests assumptions, dependencies, contradictions, sparse evidence, and downstream implications in requirements artifacts. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-spec-assumption-reviewer.md))
- **bn-spec-coherence-reviewer**: Leaf reviewer for /bn-spec-stress. Reads a requirements artifact for internal consistency -- contradictions between sections, terminology drift, structural gaps, broken references, and ambiguity where careful readers would diverge. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-spec-coherence-reviewer.md))
- **bn-spec-design-reviewer**: Leaf reviewer for /bn-spec-stress. Reads a requirements artifact for missing design decisions -- information architecture, interaction states, user-flow completeness, accessibility commitments, and AI-slop risk -- that would block or derail implementation. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-spec-design-reviewer.md))
- **bn-spec-fidelity-reviewer**: Compares a diff against its stated spec and flags work beyond the spec. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-spec-fidelity-reviewer.md))
- **bn-spec-product-reviewer**: Leaf reviewer for /bn-spec-stress. Reads a requirements artifact as a senior product leader -- challenges the premise, weighs strategic consequences, surfaces cheaper alternatives, and checks goal-requirement alignment -- while suppressing premise re-litigation when the requirements already came from /bn-brainstorm. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-spec-product-reviewer.md))
- **bn-spec-scenario-reviewer**: Leaf reviewer for /bn-spec-stress. Walks user-observable scenarios and acceptance gaps in a requirements artifact, returning only concrete missing requirements or plan inputs tied to requirement IDs and scenario triggers. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-spec-scenario-reviewer.md))
- **bn-spec-threat-reviewer**: Leaf reviewer for /bn-spec-stress. Reviews requirements-level trust, data, and misuse implications when a feature touches auth, permissions, sensitive data, external tools, or abuse surfaces. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-spec-threat-reviewer.md))
- **bn-testing-reviewer**: Always-on code-review persona. Reviews code for test coverage gaps, weak assertions, brittle implementation-coupled tests, and missing edge case coverage. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-testing-reviewer.md))
- **bn-thread-chaser**: Small recursive investigator that chases ONE thread to its leaf fact. Use when a research brief references something it never opened — a cited-but-unread migration, a half-deprecated API, a config the docs point at but never resolve — and you need the buried leaf fact verified and judged for whether it still holds. Follows the reference to its source; may spawn ONE more of itself if the thread forks into a deeper sub-thread that genuinely needs chasing and depth remains. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-thread-chaser.md))
- **bn-unit-lead**: Delivery-subtree worker that owns ONE delivery unit end to end inside its own git worktree: implements the unit on its branch, runs a test-fix loop when validation exists, spawns a scoped two-reviewer mini-review (correctness + spec fidelity) over its own diff and addresses P0/P1, then commits the unit on its branch. Splits ONCE into a child unit-lead only on genuine over-size/failure; returns blocked (never loops) if tests cannot pass. Spawned by bn-delivery-lead with isolation: worktree; never merges or pushes. ([rcmoynihan/banyan](https://github.com/rcmoynihan/banyan/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/bn-unit-lead.md))

- **bnb-baker**: Implementation agent that executes one milestone from the active run's milestones/ directory at a time, following the spec strictly and stopping at checkpoints. Use when break-n-bake workflow is in bake phase. Never runs more than one milestone per invocation without an explicit user OK. ([mbruhler/break-n-bake](https://github.com/mbruhler/break-n-bake/blob/HEAD/agents/bnb-baker.md))

([mbruhler/break-n-bake](https://github.com/mbruhler/break-n-bake/blob/HEAD/agents/bnb-breaker.md))
